- **cell-barcode-calling-and-filtering**: Use when raw 3' droplet scRNA-seq FASTQ (e.g., GemCode/10x data such as SRA SRP073767) must be turned into a cell-filtered gene-barcode UMI matrix, deciding which barcodes represent cells versus empty droplets. - **cell-barcode-demultiplexing**: Use when raw 10x-structured nanopore or PacBio long-read FASTQ reads need cell barcodes found, error-corrected against the 10x v3 whitelist, and assigned per read before alignment/quantification. - **cell-barcode-extraction-from-short-reads**: Use when reads are FASTQ/FASTA and a known sequence of interest (barcode, fusion, variant) must be located with tolerated sequencing errors, and reads must be assigned to cell barcodes for single-cell quantification or genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-extraction-from-short-reads))
- **cell-barcode-extraction-scrna-seq**: Use when processing raw 3'-end tag scRNA-seq FASTQ (e.g., MCA GSE108097 or HCL GSE134355) where cell barcodes/UMIs must be extracted from merged inserts and corrected against a whitelist before isoform quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-extraction-scrna-seq))
- **cell-barcode-posterior-error-correction**: Use when raw 10x Genomics droplet scRNA-seq FASTQ reads must have their 16 bp cell barcodes error-corrected (or discarded) prior to UMI filtering and building a gene-barcode count matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-posterior-error-correction))
- **cell-barcode-sharing-hypergeometric-testing**: Use when multiplexed 10x Genomics droplet scRNA-seq samples (e.g. HiSeq 4000-sequenced libraries) must be tested pairwise for more shared cell barcodes than expected by chance, to flag barcode-swapping artefacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-sharing-hypergeometric-testing))
- **cell-barcode-umi-extraction**: Use when processing HIT-scISOseq CCS reads where primer-based FLNC classification must also yield per-read cellBC and UMI (isoseq_flnc.BarcodeUMI.fastq), or when corrected barcodes/UMIs are needed for isoform count matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-barcode-umi-extraction))
- **cell-barcode-umi-extraction-specification**: Use when sequencing reads embed cell barcodes and UMIs at variable-length or non-fixed positions (e.g. 10x Chromium v2/v3, concatenated barcodes) and must be normalized to a simple fixed-position geometry before mapping and quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-umi-extraction-specification))
- **cell-barcode-umi-quantification**: Use when you have 10x CellRanger output (filtered_feature_bc_matrix, possorted_genome_bam.bam) and need per-cell pathogen UMI counts for an infected scRNA-seq sample, e.g. HIV EGFP+ cell detection. - **cell-barcode-whitelist-correction**: Use when demultiplexing HIT-scISOseq FLNC BarcodeUMI fastq reads into per-cell cDNA reads and observed 10x cell barcodes must be corrected against a whitelist with posterior-probability thresholds. - **cell-barcode-whitelist-matching**: Use when single-cell amplicon FASTQ data contain a fixed cell barcode+UMI pattern in R1 (e.g. 16bp barcode + 10bp UMI) and a known cell barcode whitelist is available that extracted barcodes must be matched against. - **cell-biology-verification-intermediate**: The skill leverages microscopy and follows best practices established in the biology community. ([NeuralBlitz/Agent-Gateway](https://github.com/NeuralBlitz/Agent-Gateway/tree/HEAD/agent-gateway/skills/user/cell-biology-verification-intermediate) · ★1)
- **cell-biology-verification-research-level**: The skill leverages microscopy and follows best practices established in the biology community. ([NeuralBlitz/Agent-Gateway](https://github.com/NeuralBlitz/Agent-Gateway/tree/HEAD/agent-gateway/skills/user/cell-biology-verification-research-level) · ★1)
- **cell-boundary-intersection-over-min-area-quantification**: Use when segmentation was run on overlapping image patches and you need to quantify whether per-patch masks conflict at patch seams before merging them into final cell polygons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-boundary-intersection-over-min-area-quantification))
- **cell-boundary-polygon-export**: Use when a Sopa-processed SpatialData object (e.g. a tuto.zarr store) must be converted into Xenium Explorer input files — experiment.xenium, pyramidal .ome.tif image, cell boundary polygons, and cell category files — for visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-boundary-polygon-export))
- **cell-by-bin-matrix-construction**: Use when raw scATAC-seq fragment/BAM data must be converted into a snap-format cell-by-bin count matrix (e.g., at 5-kb resolution) as the starting feature representation for clustering or dimensionality reduction without population-level peak calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-by-bin-matrix-construction))
- **cell-by-cluster-enrichment-scoring**: Use when an AnnData object has log-cpm normalised scRNA-seq data with tentative (ideally over-clustered) Leiden labels and you need per-cell enrichment scores against every cluster's marker signature to support significance-based cluster merging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-by-cluster-enrichment-scoring))
- **cell-by-feature-matrix-construction**: Use when you have a per-cell sparse k-mer abundance matrix (from jellyfish counts) and a CKG look-up table (from simhash), and need a normalized cells-by-CKGs feature matrix for downstream cell type classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-by-feature-matrix-construction))
- **cell-by-peak-anndata-construction**: Use when single-cell calling cards qbed insertions and called peaks (e.g., 902 Brd4 peaks from mouse cortex GSE148448) must be converted into a cell-by-peak count matrix for integration with Scanpy gene-expression AnnData. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-by-peak-anndata-construction))
- **cell-calling-method-benchmarking**: Use when a raw unfiltered gene-by-barcode count matrix (mtx or h5) from droplet-based scRNA/snRNA-seq needs cell-calling, and multiple callers must be compared against ground-truth labels (HTO singlet/doublet or demuxlet genotypes). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-calling-method-benchmarking))
- **cell-capacity-report**: Produce a read-only, per-cell capacity report from AMP metrics — DB count, page-cache commit, and disk used against the A12 caps — and recommend add-cell vs rebalance plus flag tenants projected to outgrow a node. Use before scaling a geo, during weekly capacity review, or when a placement looks tight. ([yashyaadav/arcadedb](https://github.com/yashyaadav/arcadedb/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cell-capacity-report) · ★1)
- **Cell-Cell Communication Analysis (CellChat)**: ✅ **Use when:** - You have an annotated scRNA-seq dataset (Seurat object with cell type labels) - You want to identify ligand-receptor interactions between cell types - You want to visualize communication networks (chord diagrams, bubble plots) - You want to find dominant sender/receiver cell populations - **Chains from** `scrnaseq-seurat-core-analysis` output (`seurat_processed.rds`) ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/omicsclaw/cell-cell-communication) · ★224 · 6 copies)
- **cell-cell-communication**: Infer ligand-receptor signaling between cell types from scRNA-seq (and spatial) data. TRIGGER when the user mentions CellChat, CellPhoneDB, LIANA, NicheNet, Squidpy, "ligand-receptor", "cell-cell communication", "CCC", "intercellular signaling", "macrophage-T-cell crosstalk", "M1/M2 signaling", or has annotated single-cell data and wants to know which cell types are talking to which. ([miller-jensen-lab/claude-code-marketplace](https://github.com/miller-jensen-lab/claude-code-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/mjlab/skills/cell-cell-communication))
- **cell-cell-communication**: Infer ligand-receptor signaling networks from annotated single-cell RNA-seq data and produce publication-ready communication plots. Use when you have a Seurat object with cell type labels and want to map sender/receiver/mediator roles, pathway activity, or cell-cell interaction matrices. ([UViiVe/fleet-skills-public](https://github.com/UViiVe/fleet-skills-public/tree/HEAD/cell-cell-communication))
- **cell-cell-communication-benchmarking**: Use when comparing ligand-receptor scoring rules for cell-cell communication inference on scRNA-seq data and you need an apples-to-apples benchmark on the same interaction database, same input matrix, and same permutation/significance pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-communication-benchmarking))
- **cell-cell-communication-inference-from-scrnaseq**: Use when scRNA-seq data has cluster/cell-type annotations and the goal is to compute transcriptome-based communication scores between sender and receiver clusters and rank specific ligand-receptor pairs (e.g., checkpoints, chemokines). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-communication-inference-from-scrnaseq))
- **cell-cell-communication-matrix-factorization**: Use when you have scRNA-seq expression (genes x cells) plus cell labels and need species-agnostic cell-cell communication inference, especially when no curated L-R database exists for the species or condition-specific shared vs unique signals are of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-communication-matrix-factorization))
- **cell-cell-communication-method-benchmarking**: Use when single-cell RNA-Seq data must be interrogated for ligand-receptor interactions with multiple CCC methods, or when per-method interaction scores need to be combined into a robust consensus ranking via LIANA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-communication-method-benchmarking))
- **cell-cell-communication-module-interpretation**: Use when you have pre-computed ligand-receptor CCI predictions (e.g., from CellChat on a scRNA-seq dataset such as GSE72056 melanoma) and need to biologically interpret which interaction groups/signals characterize a specific sender or receiver cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-communication-module-interpretation))
- **cell-cell-communication-network-analysis**: Use when you have clustered scRNA-seq cell groups and need to infer significant ligand-receptor communication between them, quantify pathway-level signaling, identify dominant senders/receivers, and group coordinated outgoing/incoming patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-communication-network-analysis))
- **cell-cell-communication-network-inference**: Use when single-cell-resolution spatial transcriptomics data (e.g., seqFISH+ mouse cortex, Seq-Scope mouse colon) with a gene expression matrix plus cell spatial coordinates and cell-type labels is available, and the goal is to estimate which neighboring cell types regulate HVG expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-communication-network-inference))
- **cell-cell-communication-score-computation**: Use when you need to quantify communication potential between an input cell population and partner cell types from transcriptomic data, e.g. comparing outward signaling of CAF-S1 vs CAF-S4 or LPS-stimulated DCs against the Primary Cell Atlas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-communication-score-computation))
- **cell-cell-distance-based-communication-analysis**: Use when you have non-spatial scRNA-seq data plus a limited spatial measurement of a few genes, and need to infer cell-cell communication or the spatial distance/range of a signaling pathway (e.g. Wg ~100 µm, Dpp ~125 µm). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-distance-based-communication-analysis))
- **cell-cell-distance-inference-from-transport-plan**: Use when you have a fitted SpaOTsc transport plan γ* (n cells × m spatial positions) plus a spatial position distance matrix, and you need a pairwise spatial cell-cell distance matrix for downstream signaling or clustering analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-distance-inference-from-transport-plan))
- **cell-cell-interaction-expression-profiling**: Use when you have developmental series scRNA-seq (e.g., GSE153164 corticogenesis) with inferred L-R pair expression-change values and need to group interactions by temporal activity to pinpoint key developmental time points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-interaction-expression-profiling))
- **cell-cell-interaction-label-aggregation**: Use when training or benchmarking a supervised CCI inference model (e.g., DeepCCI's ResNet+GCN interaction model) and per-method significant-interaction calls must be merged into a single consensus label set for classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-interaction-label-aggregation))
- **cell-cell-interaction-network-comparison**: Use when validating AI-inferred single-cell data against a real-profiled ground-truth cohort: you have matched (semi-profiled vs real-profiled) single-cell datasets with shared cell-type annotations and need a quantitative fidelity metric for cell-cell interaction inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-interaction-network-comparison))
- **cell-cell-interaction-network-degree-analysis**: Use when bulk transcriptome + survival data have been reduced to prognostic immune marker gene pairs (IMGPs) and you need to rank cell types by favorable vs unfavorable prognostic associations via network degree. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-interaction-network-degree-analysis))
- **cell-cell-interaction-network-inference**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq expression matrix, cell-type labels, and time-point annotations and need to infer which ligand-receptor interactions connect which sender/receiver cell types at a given time point. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-interaction-network-inference))
- **cell-cell-interaction-risk-score-modeling**: Use when bulk transcriptomes with matched survival data (e.g., TCGA SKCM06 plus GSE validation cohorts) are available and a prognostic model must aggregate interaction-linked marker gene pairs into one risk score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-interaction-risk-score-modeling))
- **cell-cell-signaling-perturbation-scoring**: Use when comparing cell–cell signaling between two tissue systems (e.g., IFN-stimulated vs. control PBMCs from SeuratData 'ifnb') and you need to rank edges where ligand and receptor are both significantly perturbed, whether concordantly or discordantly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-signaling-perturbation-scoring))
- **cell-citation**: Use to convert references to Cell Press author–date style — in-text "(Smith et al., 2021)", an alphabetical reference list by first author with full author lists, journal names spelled out. This is the OPPOSITE of Science's numbered-by-appearance style. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cell-Skills/skills/cell-citation) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **cell-classification-cross-tabulation**: Use when two cell-level AML/tumor classification labels exist for the same barcodes (e.g., CNA-based CONICSmat clusters vs. SNV/expression-based genotype calls) and you must quantify how well one predicts the other. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-classification-cross-tabulation))
- **cell-cluster-marker-transposon-identification**: Use when you have a processed scRNA-Seq BAM file and need to find which TEs (at locus resolution) are differentially expressed markers of specific cell clusters, e.g. cancer vs normal cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cluster-marker-transposon-identification))
- **cell-clustering-ari-evaluation**: Use when you have simulated or labeled scRNA-seq data with known cell-type labels and need to quantify how well a normalization/confounding-removal method (e.g., scPLS) preserves cluster structure via ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-clustering-ari-evaluation))
- **cell-clustering-validation-with-marker-genes**: Use when a scRNA-seq clustering result (e.g., from scSPARKL on a mixed-species sample like the Jurkat–293T mixture) must be checked against known marker genes and a ground-truth cell partition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-clustering-validation-with-marker-genes))
- **cell-code-review**: Review a dewstack tutorial's runnable code — every sql cell=, py cell=, app= cell, and every site= HTML/CSS/JS block — for pedagogical code quality: semantic names over single letters, comments that explain why rather than restate what, read in the context of the surrounding prose. Use when asked to review, clean up, or improve code quality/naming/comments in one or more tutorials, or after writing new tutorial code that should be checked before it ships. ([deweydex/dewstack](https://github.com/deweydex/dewstack/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cell-code-review))
- **cell-collective-database-model-retrieval**: Use when a refined GRN (adjacency matrix) must be converted into a draft Boolean Network and curated Boolean functions are available in the Cell Collective for the organism under study. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-collective-database-model-retrieval))
- **cell-communication**: Workflow for ligand-receptor communication inference in single-cell or spatial data with sender-receiver summaries and cautious interpretation. ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bioclaw_hub/cell-communication) · ★224 · 2 copies)
- **cell-communication**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/Single_Cell/bioSkills/cell-communication) · ★36 · 6 copies)
- **cell-communication**: Cell-cell / ligand-receptor communication analysis for single-cell data using LIANA+ (recommended consensus default), CellPhoneDB, CellChat (R), and squidpy's ligrec. Use for inferring cell-cell communication, ligand-receptor pairs, source-&gt;target signaling from an annotated .h5ad. Trigger terms - "cell-cell communication", "ligand-receptor", "CellPhoneDB", "CellChat", "LIANA", "cell interaction", "ligrec". ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-communication) · ★13)
- **cell-communication-cellphonedb**: Analyze cell-cell communication using CellPhoneDB statistical method to identify significant ligand-receptor interactions between cell types via permutation testing. ([chenyhvvvv/STAT-agent](https://github.com/chenyhvvvv/STAT-agent/tree/HEAD/src/stat_agent/skills/cell-communication-CellPhoneDB) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **cell-communication-gene-labeling**: Use when an HVG list (e.g. from FindVariableFeatures(), variance, scannpy, or M3drop on seqFISH+/Seq-Scope data) must be split into communication-related (Y) vs non-related (N) genes via GeneCards/GO labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-communication-gene-labeling))
- **cell-communication-liana**: Analyze cell-cell communication using LIANA+ to identify significant ligand-receptor interactions between cell types. (Recommended!) ([chenyhvvvv/STAT-agent](https://github.com/chenyhvvvv/STAT-agent/tree/HEAD/src/stat_agent/skills/cell-communication-LIANA) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **cell-communication-network-extraction**: Use when you have a CPM-normalized single-cell expression matrix plus a cell-annotation metafile and want to predict which ligand–receptor pairs mediate communication between annotated cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-communication-network-extraction))
- **cell-communication-statistics-summary**: Use when a single-cell atlas (e.g. Tabula Muris, ~117 cell types) has been processed with NATMI and you need organism-wide summary metrics of autocrine signalling, such as the median number of co-detected ligand–receptor pairs per cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-communication-statistics-summary))
- **cell-composition-differential-testing-along-pseudotime**: Decision support for detecting condition- or covariate-associated changes in cell abundance/density along a multi-sample scRNA-seq pseudotime trajectory (Lamian TCD/XCD testing). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-composition-differential-testing-along-pseudotime))
- **cell-connectivity-estimation-from-single-cell-data**: Use when you have a set of minimum spanning trees (one per Totem/CLARA clustering, constructed with Slingshot) and need per-cell connectivity values in [0,1] to rank clusterings and identify likely trajectory leaves and branch points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-connectivity-estimation-from-single-cell-data))
- **cell-connectivity-profiling**: Use when the input is a single-cell expression dataset (SingleCellExperiment) expected to follow a linear, bifurcating, or multifurcating tree trajectory, and you need to rank candidate clusterings and locate trajectory leaves and branch points without a prior clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-connectivity-profiling))
- **cell-connectivity-summary-edge-aggregation**: Use when NATMI ExtractEdges.py output edges exist and the goal is to summarise the entire communication network to show how strongly or specifically each cell type communicates to every other cell type, identifying highly communicating cell-type pairs or communities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-connectivity-summary-edge-aggregation))
- **cell-connectivity-vector-analysis**: Use when a Totem analysis has produced many candidate CLARA clusterings and you must automatically pick which one(s) to feed into Slingshot MST construction based on cell connectivity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-connectivity-vector-analysis))
- **cell-count-scaling-benchmark-analysis**: Use when you must show how reconstruction accuracy and sensitivity of a scGRN method depend on the number of input cells, and ground-truth regulatory edges (e.g., SERGIO-simulated 100-gene modules) are available for scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-count-scaling-benchmark-analysis))
- **cell-count-subsampling-scalability-analysis**: Use when you must quantify the runtime, memory, and clustering-performance scaling of an integration/clustering method across increasing numbers of cells and compare it with alternatives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-count-subsampling-scalability-analysis))
- **cell-count-summary-reporting**: Use when a VDJ preprocessing or filtering step (e.g. setupVdjPseudobulk) has just been applied and you must quantify and report how many cells were retained versus input, as a baseline check before downstream pseudobulking or trajectory analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-count-summary-reporting))
- **cell-coverage-outlier-filtering**: Use when a raw single-cell ChIP-seq/scATAC-seq count matrix (cells x bins/peaks/TSS regions) contains low-quality or extreme-coverage cells that must be filtered before PCA, t-SNE/UMAP, or clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-coverage-outlier-filtering))
- **cell-crowding-visualization**: Use when evaluating whether a low-dimensional embedding of small UMI count matrices suffers from cell crowding at the latent origin, comparing vMF spherical vs Euclidean VAE latents or t-SNE/UMAP/PHATE baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-crowding-visualization))
- **cell-culture-basics**: Mammalian cell culture fundamentals — aseptic technique, passaging, cryopreservation, and contamination control. ([aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills](https://github.com/aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-culture-basics) · ★68 · 3 copies)
- **cell-culture-best-practices**: Use when applying cell culture best practices. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-culture-best-practices) · ★2)
- **cell-culture-fundamentals**: Use when applying cell culture fundamentals. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-culture-fundamentals) · ★2)
- **cell-culture-implementation**: Use when applying cell culture implementation. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-culture-implementation) · ★2)
- **cell-culture-tech**: Expert cell culture technician with deep knowledge of aseptic technique, mammalian cell maintenance, passaging protocols, cryopreservation, and contamination troubleshooting. Specializes in HEK293, HeLa, CHO, and primary cells. Use when: cell-culture, aseptic-technique, cell-passaging, tissue-culture, mycoplasma-testing. ([theneoai/awesome-skills](https://github.com/theneoai/awesome-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/persona/research/cell-culture-tech) · ★204 · 2 copies)
- **cell-culture-troubleshooting**: Use when applying cell culture troubleshooting. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-culture-troubleshooting) · ★2)
- **cell-curator**: Annotate pre-clustered AnnData or MuData from local marker, ontology, reference, and multimodal evidence. Use for review-gated cell-type annotation, uncertainty assessment, parent-scoped refinement, and guarded write-back. ([mdmanurung/cell-curator](https://github.com/mdmanurung/cell-curator/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-curator))
- **cell-cycle-age-distribution-modeling**: Use when flow-cytometric single-cell data from an unsynchronized, exponentially growing culture must be placed on real cell-cycle time, and no time-resolved sampling exists to calibrate pseudotime directly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-age-distribution-modeling))
- **cell-cycle-analysis**: Cell cycle phase scoring and visualization for single-cell expression data. Uses Scanpy's score_genes_cell_cycle with Tirosh et al. 2015 S/G2M gene lists on any normalized AnnData (ThreePrimeUntargeted via Loader, scRNA-seq-like, or AnnData from CSV/parquet count matrix). Regressing out cell cycle effects for downstream analysis. Use when the user asks about cell cycle, proliferation, S phase, G2M, or cell state. ([Elembio/agent-skills](https://github.com/Elembio/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/multiomics/skills/cell-cycle-analysis))
- **cell-cycle-effect-removal**: Decision support for removing cell-cycle effects from single-cell RNA-seq expression matrices when an annotated set of cell-cycle control genes is available and downstream analyses require corrected expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-effect-removal--1c3d2484aab0bf12))
- **cell-cycle-effect-removal**: Use when a scRNA-Seq expression matrix (log-scaled, gene-by-gene centered) shows within-cell-type heterogeneity driven by annotated cell-cycle genes that obscures clustering of cell types or substructure, and a known cell-cycle gene set can be retrieved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-effect-removal--2fb76049d54d8e4e))
- **cell-cycle-effect-removal-method-benchmarking**: Use when scRNA-seq data contain cell-cycle effects confounding subclone or subpopulation delineation, and the goal is to quantify which correction approach (circular vs linear) maximizes subclone separability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-effect-removal-method-benchmarking))
- **cell-cycle-gene-annotation-mapping**: Use when preparing a gene-by-cell expression matrix for ccRemover: gene names must be mapped to human ('human') or mouse ('mouse') cell-cycle annotations to build the if_cc indicator vector before running ccRemover. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-gene-annotation-mapping))
- **cell-cycle-gene-list-curation**: Use when preparing DeepCycle inputs: you need an organism-specific cell-cycle gene list, a base cycling gene (Nusap1 for mESCs, MELK for human fibroblasts), and expression/filter thresholds for gene inclusion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-gene-list-curation))
- **cell-cycle-gene-phase-annotation**: Use when single cells have been reordered along a differentiation trajectory (pseudo-temporal position) and you must cluster gene expression dynamics into temporal waves and check whether cell-cycle gene sets (mitosis vs DNA synthesis) fall into distinct, out-of-phase clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-gene-phase-annotation))
- **cell-cycle-gene-regression-preprocessing**: Use when applying UniTVelo to a pancreas AnnData with spliced/unspliced counts where a known subtle fate transition (e.g., Fev+ to Delta) must be recovered both with and without cell-cycle gene regression, to match a reference fate-mapping result. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-gene-regression-preprocessing))
- **cell-cycle-gene-set-factor-analysis**: Use when labeled (Hoechst or equivalent) scRNA-seq or qPCR single-cell data are available and the goal is to quantify how accurately different cell-cycle characterization methods recover cell-cycle phase labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-gene-set-factor-analysis))
- **cell-cycle-geneset-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when you need to show that periodic genes discovered ab initio from a circular manifold (Cyclum pseudo-time) are more enriched for GO:0007049 cell-cycle terms than genes ranked by principal components on scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-geneset-enrichment-interpretation))
- **cell-cycle-marker-expression-analysis**: Use when a velocity-informed circular trajectory (e.g., Cytopath on the U2OS SMART-Seq2 cell cycle dataset) has been inferred and G1-phase cells must be split into early vs late stages and validated with marker expression tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-marker-expression-analysis))
- **cell-cycle-nicotine-target-nomination**: Use when comparing a treated vs. control single-cell RNA-seq dataset (e.g., 10X Chromium hESC, ~12,280 cells) to find genes whose periodic/circular expression kinetics change between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-nicotine-target-nomination))
- **cell-cycle-phase-binning**: Use when h5ad data carry a continuous DeepCycle phase annotation (adata.obs['cell_cycle_theta']) with Ms/Mu layers, and you need to quantify how per-cell RNA content (total UMIs) changes around the cell cycle or locate phase transitions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-binning))
- **cell-cycle-phase-classification**: Use when a processed Seurat object or count matrix from single-cell/spatial RNA-seq needs per-cell cell cycle phase assignment (G0, G1, S, G2M), e.g. to quantify quiescent fractions alongside accordion() cell type annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-classification))
- **cell-cycle-phase-classification-svm**: Use when you have single-cell imaging (4i) or single-cell RNA-seq data with a top-k ranked feature subset and ground-truth cell cycle phase labels, and need an accuracy-based comparison of feature selection methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-classification-svm))
- **cell-cycle-phase-correlation-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq data exhibit dominant cell cycling at the onset of differentiation and you need to check whether RNA velocity-based simulation trajectories recapitulate cell cycle stages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-correlation-analysis))
- **cell-cycle-phase-inference-deep-learning**: Use when scRNA-seq data contains spliced/unspliced layers, cells are proliferating, and a continuous cell cycle phase assignment is needed from RNA velocity dynamics rather than gene-marker scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-inference-deep-learning))
- **cell-cycle-phase-scoring-tricycle**: Use when you need to evaluate whether forecast or observed single-cell transcriptomic states recapitulate cell-cycle progression, by mapping cells (genes x cells matrices, e.g. scEU-seq hTERT RPE data) to tricycle phase positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-scoring-tricycle))
- **cell-cycle-phase-transition-estimation**: Use when an h5ad annotated by DeepCycle (with obs['cell_cycle_theta'] and scVelo Ms/Mu layers) needs cell-cycle phase labels and per-phase cell fractions, e.g. to validate inferred thetas against flow-cytometry proportions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-transition-estimation))
- **cell-cycle-pseudo-time-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq gene expression is expected to follow a periodic process (e.g., cell cycle) and you need ab initio per-cell pseudo-time or phase ordering without prior marker-gene lists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-pseudo-time-inference))
- **cell-cycle-pseudo-time-reconstruction**: Use when given an unsynchronized scRNA-seq expression matrix (log2 TPM+1) and the goal is to recover cell cycle ordering (G1, S, G2/M) or compare pseudo-time methods such as SCUBA, Monocle, TSCAN, Wishbone, or DPT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-pseudo-time-reconstruction))
- **cell-cycle-pseudotime-evaluation**: Use when evaluating an ordering/pseudo-time method for cyclic single-cell data against known stage labels (e.g., G1/S/G2/M), by sweeping cluster number k and comparing correlation-score curves between a proposed method and baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-pseudotime-evaluation))
- **cell-cycle-score-clustering**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq dataset may contain quiescent G0-like cells that confound pseudotime reconstruction, and cell-cycle scores (e.g. reCAT mean scores) are available to identify and remove them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-score-clustering))
- **cell-cycle-scoring-single-cell**: Use when a projected single-cell query contains predicted cycling cells (e.g. Mki67+ exhausted T cells) that need to be quantified and visualized against the projected cell-state labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-scoring-single-cell))
- **cell-cycle-stage-comparison**: Use when you have single-cell Hi-C contact matrices resolved by cell-cycle stage and need to test whether nested TLD hierarchy (modularity, TLD-adjR2, weighted similarity WS) changes across stages such as G1 vs S phase vs G2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-stage-comparison))
- **cell-cycle-stage-interpretation-of-marker-genes**: Use when you need to demonstrate or verify that individual cell cycle marker genes cannot classify single cells into cycle stages, motivating a multi-gene, genome-wide approach such as reCAT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-stage-interpretation-of-marker-genes))
- **cell-cycle-variation-removal**: Use when single-cell count matrices show cell-cycle-driven confounding (per-cell cell-cycle scores/phases available) that must be removed while preserving biological signal such as subclone or cell-type separability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-variation-removal))
- **cell-data**: Use to build Cell's data and code deposition plan and the Data and Code Availability statement that lives inside STAR Methods Resource Availability — approved repositories, accessions/DOIs at submission, and Cell's three-bullet availability format with Mendeley Data as Elsevier's default. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cell-Skills/skills/cell-data) · ★1.4k · 4 copies)
- **cell-density-convex-hull-coverage-quantification**: Use when evaluating scRNA-seq embeddings for trajectory inference suitability and you need a quantitative cell-density metric: cells should be well-distributed (neither over-clumped nor over-dispersed) in the 2D embedding before fitting pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-density-convex-hull-coverage-quantification))
- **cell-design**: Use when generating a design OR when ANY change alters the architecture — a component added, removed, or renamed; an edge or dependency changed; exposure changed; an external/SaaS dependency added or dropped. specs/design/design.cell moves FIRST, before the rest of the design (domain-model.md, flows/, the component design.json files). Covers the grammar, the AEP boundary semantics (where each dependency goes), and the write protocol that drives the live architecture diagram. ([wso2/labs-agentic-engineer](https://github.com/wso2/labs-agentic-engineer/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-design) · ★25)
- **cell-detection**: Cell segmentation in fluorescence microscopy images. Supports Cellpose/cpsam (Cellpose 4.0) with additional backends planned. Produces segmentation masks, per-cell morphology metrics (area, diameter, centroid, eccentricity), overlay figures, and a report.md. ([ClawBio/ClawBio](https://github.com/ClawBio/ClawBio/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-detection) · ★1.2k · 9 copies)
- **cell-detection**: Comprehensive guide to cell detection. Master the concepts, implementation, best practices, and real-world applications of cell detection in professional environments. ([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/cell-detection) · ★1)
- **cell-diagram**: Use when asked to draw, write, or edit a cell architecture diagram or a `.cell` file, when working with Cell Architect or @kanushka/cell-diagram-react, or when a system needs to be shown as a cell with what it exposes and what it depends on. ([kanushka/cell-architect](https://github.com/kanushka/cell-architect/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-diagram) · ★5)
- **cell-dimension-value-extraction**: Use when processing natural-language prokaryotic taxonomic descriptions to populate a taxon-by-character matrix with cell dimension (diameter, width, length) measurement states and units. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cell-dimension-value-extraction))
- **cell-divide**: Reproductive function. Copies the root .Codex/skills/ genome into a target section's .Codex/skills/ location (or deeper), appends the parent commit SHA to each child SKILL.md lineage, and registers the new cell in lattice/knowledge/skills_registry. INERT in Phase 1 — do not execute recursive division yet. ([JeromyJSmith/lattice-platform](https://github.com/JeromyJSmith/lattice-platform/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cell-divide) · ★3)
- **cell-divide**: Reproductive function. Copies the root .claude/skills/ genome into a target section's .claude/skills/ location (or deeper), appends the parent commit SHA to each child SKILL.md lineage, and registers the new cell in lattice/knowledge/skills_registry. INERT in Phase 1 — do not execute recursive division yet. ([JeromyJSmith/lattice-platform](https://github.com/JeromyJSmith/lattice-platform/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cell-divide) · ★3)
- **cell-embedding-reliability-classification**: Use when a Seurat scRNA-seq/CyTOF object has been embedded with t-SNE or UMAP and you need to quantify which cell embeddings are dubious vs trustworthy, or to pick hyperparameters (perplexity, min.dist, n.neighbors) by minimizing dubious embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-embedding-reliability-classification))
- **cell-explore**: Explore Virtuoso cellviews - list libraries, cells, instances, nets, layers, and hierarchy. Use when browsing a design, understanding circuit topology, or inspecting layout/schematic contents. ([deanyou/virtuoso-cli](https://github.com/deanyou/virtuoso-cli/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cell-explore) · ★45 · 4 copies)
- **cell-fate-branch-allocation-quantification**: Use when scRNA-seq cells from perturbed (e.g. knockout GMP) populations must be allocated to branches of an already-inferred wild-type branching trajectory to measure fate bias between, e.g., monocyte and granulocyte branches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-branch-allocation-quantification))
- **cell-fate-dynamical-analysis**: Use when a validated biomolecular network model must be simulated via dynamical analysis to obtain cell fate outcomes (normal proliferation, apoptosis), or when its robustness to small input perturbations must be quantified by SEM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-fate-dynamical-analysis))
- **cell-fate-dynamical-manifold-construction**: Use when you have a cell-cell rwTPM and DynamicalAnalysis output (attractor basins, coarse-grained P_ij, soft membership rho*(x)) from MuTrans and need to visualize the manifold, place attractor basins and saddle/transition cells on it, or sanity-check basin structure before lineage (MPPT) or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-dynamical-manifold-construction))
- **cell-fate-fraction-quantification**: Use when you have simulated single-cell trajectories from a PRESCIENT perturbation run (e.g., TF overexpression/knockdown sweeps) and need to measure how perturbation magnitude shifts fate fractions at the terminal time point, with statistical testing of reciprocal fate changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-fraction-quantification))
- **cell-fate-lineage-classification**: Use when you have GEMLI-predicted lineage assignments plus cell type annotations (e.g., DCIS vs inv_tumor cells from GSE148093 breast cancer biopsies) and need to separate symmetric from asymmetric (mixed-fate) lineages and profile DEGs per fate class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-lineage-classification))
- **cell-fate-probability-inference**: Use when a scRNA-seq task asks to transfer continuous (non-discrete) annotations such as 7-dimensional cell fate probability vectors between two sequencing runs of the Tusi hematopoietic progenitor dataset, and to rank annotation-transfer methods by JSD. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-probability-inference))
- **cell-fate-probability-normalization**: Use when RNA velocity (scvelo spliced/unspliced AnnData) plus Cytopath consensus trajectory steps are available and you need per-cell alignment scores, trajectory assignments, pseudotime, fate probabilities, or differentiation potential per cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-probability-normalization))
- **cell-fate-probability-quantification**: Use when scRNA-seq data with cluster annotations and an RNA velocity field are available and you need per-cell probabilities of reaching defined terminal fates (e.g., alpha, beta, epsilon) plus aggregate end-fate frequencies from stochastic Markov sampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-probability-quantification))
- **cell-fate-probability-validation-pancreatic-endocrine**: Use when simulating cell-fate decisions from a preprocessed pancreatic endocrine AnnData with cluster annotations and differentiation potentials, and RNA velocity is unreliable, so trajectories must be biased by the differentiation-potential gradient toward terminal fates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-probability-validation-pancreatic-endocrine))
- **cell-fate-propensity-quantification**: Use when a validated biomolecular regulatory network (e.g., the 197-node/744-edge Cho et al. network) has been simulated with CanSeer's DA and stable-state probabilities must be converted into per-fate propensity values for validation or treatment comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-fate-propensity-quantification))
- **cell-fate-terminal-state-detection-evaluation**: Use when a single-cell trajectory dataset may harbor elusive or low-population terminal fates (e.g., multifurcating, cyclic, or disconnected topologies) and you need automated terminal-state calling plus a quantitative fate-recovery score against known labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-terminal-state-detection-evaluation))
- **cell-fate-transition-scoring**: Use when evaluating RNA velocity or latent-trajectory outputs on a multi-lineage, multi-batch scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., mouse gastrulation AnnData with spliced/unspliced layers) and you need quantitative transition, batch-mixing, and velocity-consistency scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-transition-scoring))
- **cell-favorability-score-computation**: Use when a significant directed cell-cell interaction network (e.g., cytotoxic cells → tumor cells) has already been inferred from bulk expression plus survival data with TimiGP and you need per-cell favorable/unfavorable prognostic rankings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-favorability-score-computation))
- **cell-figure-guide**: Cell (Cell Press) figure preparation: resolution (300-1000 DPI), formats (TIFF/PDF), RGB color, Avenir/Arial fonts, uppercase panel labels, strict image manipulation policies. ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/scientific-writing/cell-figure-guide) · ★394 · 5 copies)
- **cell-figure-guide**: Figure and image preparation guide for Cell (Cell Press) journal. Covers resolution (300-1000 DPI by type), file formats (TIFF, PDF), RGB color mode, Avenir/Arial fonts, uppercase panel labels, and strict image manipulation policies. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-figure-guide) · ★1)
- **cell-figures**: Use to finalize Cell Press display items — column-width sizing, minimum fonts, RGB, show-the-data with defined error bars/n/replicate type, scale bars, colorblind-safe palettes, multi-panel discipline, stand-alone legends, and image integrity. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cell-Skills/skills/cell-figures) · ★1.4k · 4 copies)
- **cell-fit**: Use when triaging a study before any writing — stress-tests whether it clears Cell's bar (a complete, mechanistic, hypothesis-driven story with multiple converging lines of evidence) or belongs at a sibling. Use this first to decide Cell vs Molecular Cell vs Cell Reports vs Cell Reports Medicine before investing in framing or figures. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cell-Skills/skills/cell-fit) · ★1.4k · 4 copies)
- **cell-fit**: Use first, before any writing, to stress-test whether a study clears Cell's bar — a complete, mechanistic, hypothesis-driven story with converging evidence. Decides Cell vs Molecular Cell vs Cell Reports vs Cell Reports Medicine. ([franklee16/academic-research-skills](https://github.com/franklee16/academic-research-skills/tree/HEAD/Awesome-Journal-Skills-main/Cell-Skills/skills/cell-fit) · ★240)
- **cell-framing**: Use to lock the single narrative arc before drafting — converts a set of correct results into one Cell-shaped story that runs hypothesis → mechanism → significance, not a list of experiments. - **cell-free-expression**: Guidance for cell-free protein synthesis (CFPS) optimization.
