- **cell-gene-matrix-extraction-from-segmentation-mask**: Use when a BIDCell segmentation mask (pixel values = cell IDs, *_connected.tif) must be converted into a per-cell transcript count matrix (expr_mat.csv) from a transcripts file such as Xenium's transcripts.csv.gz. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-gene-matrix-extraction-from-segmentation-mask))
- **cell-graph-topology-reconstruction**: Use when a scRNA-seq count matrix must be embedded in a latent space that preserves inter-cell topological/trajectory structure, not just per-cell features — e.g. clustering and trajectory inference where inter-cluster distances matter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-graph-topology-reconstruction))
- **cell-grna-assignment-annotation**: Use when a single-cell CRISPR screen needs gRNAs assigned to cell barcodes from per-cell UMI counts, especially to separate ambient from endogenously expressed guides and distinguish singlets from multiplets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-grna-assignment-annotation))
- **cell-hashing-data-analysis**: Use when validating a cell-calling method (e.g., EmptyNN) on a cell hashing dataset by asking whether retained barcodes are transcriptionally closer to confirmed cells than removed barcodes within the same cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-hashing-data-analysis))
- **cell-hashing-demultiplexing**: Use when a Seurat object carries an 'HTO' assay from a cell-hashing experiment and per-cell sample identities (singlet/doublet/negative) must be called, especially with many hashtags, imbalanced sample sizes, or degraded hashtag staining. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-hashing-demultiplexing))
- **cell-hashing-snp-label-correlation**: Use when a dataset has both cell hashing (HTO) demultiplexing labels and SNP/genotype-based demultiplexing labels (e.g. scSplit) and per-cell sample identities must be reconciled into a final assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-hashing-snp-label-correlation))
- **cell-health-check**: Use this skill when the user mentions: cell health, blast radius check, cell status, SRE runbook, check cell isolation, validate cell, cell contract review, cell observability review. Audits a cell implementation against its health contract and produces a structured report with remediation suggestions. ([ai-digital-architect/prompt-library](https://github.com/ai-digital-architect/prompt-library/tree/HEAD/cell-based-architecture/.github/skills/cell-health-check))
- **cell-highlights**: Use to produce the three signature Cell front-matter artifacts — Highlights (3–4 bullets, each ≤85 characters), the eTOC/In Brief blurb (~50 words, third-person), and the Graphical Abstract (single-panel visual summary). Late-stage polish. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cell-Skills/skills/cell-highlights) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **cell-host-and-microbe**: Use when targeting Cell Host & Microbe (Cell Host Microbe) or deciding whether a host-microbe interaction or microbiome manuscript fits this venue. Encodes the journal's fit, framing, method-and-evidence bar, house style, official-submission re-check, and desk-reject heuristics. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/English-NaturalScience-Journal-Skills/skills/cell-host-and-microbe) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **cell-image**: 将论文、课题、摘要、方法、结果或参考图转换为科学论证清晰、期刊约束明确、 可生成并可审查的科研机制图、Graphical Abstract、技术路线图、研究框架图与实验示意图。 适用于生物医学、材料化学、教育/社科、AI/计算机等领域。核心流程为： Figure Contract → Domain Router → Figure-Type Router → Journal/Policy Gate → Visual Schema → Exact-Text Prompt → Rendering → SVG Reconstruction/Export → Scientific QA → Publication QA。 ([butterflylittle/cell-image-skill](https://github.com/butterflylittle/cell-image-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★8)
- **cell-image-segmentation-with-cellprofiler**: Use when running the SpaceM workflow on MALDI-imaging mass spectrometry data from cultured cells and you need per-cell segmentation of the stitched pre-MALDI microscopy tiles to assign metabolite intensities to individual cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cell-image-segmentation-with-cellprofiler))
- **cell-instance-segmentation-eval**: This evaluation probes a model's ability to perform precise instance segmentation on challenging biomedical microscopy images. It specifically tests handling of overlapping, irregularly shaped cells across varying contrast modalities (brightfield, phase-contrast, fluorescence) and object densities. Use when the user wants to benchmark on LIVECell, EVICAN2, ISBI2014, Revvity-25, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports AP (Average Precision). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/cell-instance-segmentation-eval) · ★3)
- **cell-label-entropy-computation**: Use when FFC cluster labels exist (fire_temp=100) and you need per-cell label uncertainty — e.g., to flag candidate transition cells along a pseudotime trajectory or to quantify clustering confidence in scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-label-entropy-computation))
- **cell-landmark-matrix-fusion**: Use when scRNA-seq data have already been reduced by scQA's LC1 and LC2 modules into qualitative (Q1, binary) and quantitative (Q2) cell-landmark matrices and cells must be clustered without predetermining the number of cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-landmark-matrix-fusion))
- **cell-line-cluster-assignment-scoring**: Use when cells from a scATAC-seq or scChIP-seq dataset have known labels (cell line, replicate, sample) and clusters computed by ChromSCape must be scored for label concordance or compared between samples with Fisher's exact test. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-line-cluster-assignment-scoring))
- **cell-line-drug-sensitivity-benchmarking**: Use when comparing drug-responsive cell-type/cell-line inference tools on cancer cell line scRNA-seq data with known sensitivity labels (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-line-drug-sensitivity-benchmarking))
- **cell-line-enhancer-sequence-processing**: Use when enhancer training/test FASTA sequences for one of the eight human cell lines must be converted into dna2vec and motif-based features before running Enhancer-MDLF (main.py) training or prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-line-enhancer-sequence-processing))
- **cell-line-medium-configuration**: Use when predicting metabolic fluxes from cancer cell-line gene expression (e.g., NCI-60) with METAFlux and a cell-culture medium constraint must be chosen or customized (nutrient presence vs absence). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-line-medium-configuration))
- **cell-line-panel-filtering-b-cell-cancer**: Use when a GDSC1000-style pharmacogenomics screen must be subset to a defined cancer-type cell-line panel (e.g., B-cell cancers) before grouping drug responses (S/R/N) and computing percent coverage of drug-combination regimens. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-line-panel-filtering-b-cell-cancer))
- **cell-line-pharmacogenomic-data-joining**: Use when CMS class labels from CMScaller must be merged with published per-cell-line drug response data (e.g., 5-FU IC50) in MSS colorectal cancer models to test whether subtypes predict drug response. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-line-pharmacogenomic-data-joining))
- **cell-line-similarity-analysis**: Use when you need to quantify how well a cell-specific enhancer model trained on one human cell line transfers to the other seven cell lines' test sets, and to rank cell line similarity from cross-prediction BACC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-line-similarity-analysis))
- **cell-line-specific-enhancer-model-interpretation**: Use when you have a trained Enhancer-MDLF model on one of the eight human cell lines and need to identify which TFBS motifs drive enhancer predictions via SHAP attribution over motif-module features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-line-specific-enhancer-model-interpretation))
- **cell-line-specific-model-training**: Use when you have fasta-formatted SE/TE sequences for one cell line (e.g. mESC) and want a cell-line-specific sequence-only classifier with user-set learning rate and epochs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-line-specific-model-training))
- **cell-lineage-branch-structure-validation**: Use when pretrained-model feature embeddings (e.g., tGPT hidden-state features) must be checked against known cell type labels and states by reconstructing developmental lineage trajectories and branch structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-lineage-branch-structure-validation))
- **cell-marker-database-querying**: Use when you have a per-cluster marker table (Entrez IDs, cluster labels, expression scores such as logFC from Seurat's FindAllMarkers) and need automated cell-type annotations from public marker databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-marker-database-querying))
- **cell-mask-binarization-and-major-axis-alignment**: Use when you have segmented single-cell masks from multiplexed tissue images and need to build the autoencoder cell shape descriptor inputs described by MIRIAM (binarize, rotate major axis horizontal, resize to 128x128). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-mask-binarization-and-major-axis-alignment))
- **cell-mask-binarization-and-resizing**: Use when segmented single-cell masks from multiplexed tissue images (e.g., MIBI or multiplexed immunofluorescence) must be converted to a fixed-size, orientation-normalized binary format for downstream deep learning shape characterization with an autoencoder. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-mask-binarization-and-resizing))
- **cell-merging-skill**: Expert knowledge for Cell Merging (Row & Column Spanning) in Syncfusion Blazor DataGrid. Use this skill for feature-implementation or bug-fix tasks scoped to manual merging (MergeCells/UnmergeCells APIs), automatic spanning (AutoSpan modes), and cross-feature interaction guarantees with frozen columns, grouping, virtualization, and rendering pipelines. ([Akileshaubit/ExcelCopyPasteWASM](https://github.com/Akileshaubit/ExcelCopyPasteWASM/tree/HEAD/blazor-grid-components/.codestudio/skills/cell-merging-skill))
- **cell-metabolism**: Use when targeting Cell Metabolism or deciding whether a metabolism/physiology manuscript fits this venue. Encodes the journal's fit, framing, method-and-evidence bar, house style, official-submission re-check, and desk-reject heuristics. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/English-NaturalScience-Journal-Skills/skills/cell-metabolism) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **cell-metadata-annotation**: Use when starting a SingCellaR workflow from a Cell Ranger gene-expression matrix and you need per-cell QC annotation (UMIs, detected genes, mitochondrial percentage) before filtering low-quality cells and doublets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-metadata-annotation))
- **cell-metadata-standardization-singlecellexperiment**: Use when a scRNA-seq SingleCellExperiment (e.g., a Bioconductor ExperimentHub anchor dataset such as EH2259, the Kang PBMC IFN-β data) has heterogeneous or missing cell-level metadata columns and must be harmonized before muscat aggregation/DS analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-metadata-standardization-singlecellexperiment))
- **cell-mode**: The front door for any task that needs rigor. Matches the task to a playbook, runs it with the project's own rules attached, and ends with evidence you can check. Use when the person types /cell-mode. The plugin's session hook also starts it. ([Celliusx3/cell-stack](https://github.com/Celliusx3/cell-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-mode) · ★1)
- **cell-morphology-metric-correlation**: Use when comparing BIDCell vs vendor 10x segmentation masks on Xenium data, or testing cross-replicate concordance of cell morphology, as part of the CellSPA category-A baseline and category agreement metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-morphology-metric-correlation))
- **cell-number-down-sampling-equalization**: Use when comparing cluster-purity (ROGUE) values across populations whose cell-type cell counts differ substantially — e.g., aggregated batches vs. individual patient samples in Kang et al. GSE96583 PBMC data — to rule out cell-count confounding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-number-down-sampling-equalization))
- **cell-number-scaling-performance-evaluation**: Use when designing or interpreting simulations that need the effect of cells-per-subpopulation-sample on differential state detection performance (TPR/FDR) quantified across aggregation-based and cell-level methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-number-scaling-performance-evaluation))
- **cell-number-subsampling-scaling-analysis**: Use when evaluating how loop-calling performance scales with the number of single cells, e.g. comparing SnapHiC on per-cell subsamples of mES scHi-C data against HiCCUPS on pooled contacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-number-subsampling-scaling-analysis))
- **cell-ontology-based-cell-type-annotation**: Use when a prepared H5AD study (e.g. the blood_covid AnnData file) needs standardized, ontology-resolved cell type labels per cell — e.g. before cross-study expression comparison in Cellenium — rather than ad hoc cluster labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-ontology-based-cell-type-annotation))
- **cell-ontology-embedding**: Use when annotating single-cell data against the Cell Ontology controlled vocabulary, especially when test labels may include unseen cell type terms that have no annotated cells in the training set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-ontology-embedding))
- **cell-ontology-graph-score-propagation**: Use when the query dataset may contain cell types absent from the training reference and scores must be produced for all Cell Ontology terms, not just seen training labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-ontology-graph-score-propagation))
- **cell-ontology-graph-similarity-computation**: Use when you need to test whether ontology-defined cell type relatedness (Cell Ontology graph structure) predicts expression-profile or text-description similarity, or to validate ontology-based annotation methods like OnClass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-ontology-graph-similarity-computation))
- **cell-ontology-hierarchy-traversal**: Use when evaluating a Cell Ontology-based cell type classifier at multiple annotation granularities, or when fine labels must be collapsed into coarser parent classes (e.g. 164 → 31 labels) using ontology-derived information content. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-ontology-hierarchy-traversal))
- **cell-ontology-label-mapping**: Use when single-cell annotations are free-text or dataset-specific terms needing mapping to Cell Ontology terms, or when labels must be predicted for CL terms absent from the training set via the ontology graph. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-ontology-label-mapping))
- **cell-ontology-score-prediction**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq cells from one or many datasets must be scored against the full Cell Ontology with a pretrained OnClass model, so the score matrix can serve as an embedding for annotation, clustering, or integration benchmarks against expression-based methods like Scanorama. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-ontology-score-prediction))
- **cell-ontology-term-counting**: Use when you need to verify or reproduce the dataset-construction statistics of CellO's SRA-derived bulk RNA-seq training set (samples, studies, CL terms, most-specific terms) from the deposited Zenodo package. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-ontology-term-counting))
- **cell-ontology-term-mapping**: Use when marker cell-type and tissue labels from heterogeneous marker databases must be harmonized onto Cell Ontology and Uberon terms before computing consistency-weighted marker scores or running accordion() annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-ontology-term-mapping))
- **cell-partition-consistency-verification**: Use when SkewC_TrimClustering.Rmd (R tclust) has produced TypicalCellsID.tsv and SkewedCellsID.tsv and you must verify the two lists cover every cell exactly once. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-partition-consistency-verification))
- **cell-phase-posterior-conditioned-gene-fitting**: Use when Step 1 phase posteriors exist for cells in a droplet/plate scRNA-seq dataset and you need to identify de novo circadian cycling genes among currently non-cycling genes before refitting the model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-phase-posterior-conditioned-gene-fitting))
- **cell-polygon-rasterization**: Use when per-cell staining-intensity (channel averaging) values must be computed for millions of cells over large multi-channel images where conventional raster-mask storage would exceed available memory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-polygon-rasterization))
- **cell-population-2d-visualization**: Use when scRNA-seq UMI count data with high dropout (e.g. PBMC3k 2700 cells x 1158 genes) needs unsupervised dimension reduction to a 2D visualization that distinguishes cell types or resolves sub-populations such as FCGR3A+ vs CD14+ monocytes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-population-2d-visualization))
- **cell-population-evolution-visualization**: Use when analyzing multi-timepoint scRNA-seq (t0/t1 expression matrices with t0 labels) and you need to display how annotated and novel cell populations evolve, e.g., emerging tumor subclones. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-population-evolution-visualization))
- **cell-population-reference-mapping**: Use when a single-cell AnnData carries multiple conflicting annotation columns (e.g., cellHarmony label transfer to Hay et al. CD34+ reference populations, Leiden at several resolutions, or RNA vs. clonal-genotype GoT subclusters) and a stable per-cell population assignment is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-population-reference-mapping))
- **cell-press**: Prepare manuscripts for Cell Press journals (Cell, Molecular Cell, Neuron, Immunity, Cell Reports, Cell Systems, iScience, Cell Metabolism, Current Biology and others), covering Summary, Highlights, eTOC blurb and graphical abstract, STAR Methods with the Key Resources Table, resource availability (lead contact, materials, data and code), figure and statistics requirements, and Cell Press formatting at submission vs revision. Use when targeting a Cell Press journal, writing highlights or a graphical abstract, or converting methods into STAR Methods. ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/KalarisLabs/research-agent-skills/skills/cell-press) · ★1.6k · 4 copies)
- **cell-press**: Shared author-guideline skill for Cell Press journals that food & nutrition researchers publish in: Cell, Cell Metabolism, Cell Host & Microbe, Molecular Cell, Immunity, Current Biology, Cell Reports Medicine, One Earth, Joule, Matter, Chem. Use to format or check a manuscript for a Cell Press journal — structure with STAR Methods, abstract + eTOC blurb, Cell Press reference style, and figure specs. Triggers: submit to Cell, Cell Metabolism, Cell Host & Microbe guidelines, Cell Press formatting. Also fires when the user wants to publish on/in this journal, format a manuscript for it, or match… ([PangenomeAI/academic-skills-food-nutrition](https://github.com/PangenomeAI/academic-skills-food-nutrition/tree/HEAD/journals/cell-press) · ★43)
- **cell-proliferation-growth-weight-estimation**: Use when training PRESCIENT on longitudinal scRNA-seq (e.g., Weinreb hematopoiesis days 2/4/6) and fate-bias or interpolation predictions must account for cell proliferation via growth weights. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-proliferation-growth-weight-estimation))
- **cell-proximity-analysis-in-reconstructed-space**: Use when scRNA-seq cells have been projected into pseudo-space via scSpace and clustered, and you must test whether a subpopulation near a reference cell type (e.g., malignant cells) shows a distinct functional signature such as T cell exhaustion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-proximity-analysis-in-reconstructed-space))
- **cell-qc-filtering-threshold-application**: Use when preparing Tabula Muris or similar scRNA-seq count matrices (genes x cells) as training/testing input for cell-type identification methods, so that low-quality cells and unannotated cells are removed with the paper's exact thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-qc-filtering-threshold-application))
- **cell-quality-control**: Cell- and gene-level QC filtering for AVITI24 data after run-quality-overview — compute_qc_metrics, filter_cells / filter_modalities for modalities actually present, and filter_genes for RNA (3′ / Transcript) before normalize. Use when filtering low-quality cells or genes, setting QC thresholds, or generating filter reports. Not for run/well rollups. Never invent modalities absent from the store. ([Elembio/agent-skills](https://github.com/Elembio/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/multiomics/skills/cell-quality-control))
- **cell-quality-filtering-umi-counts**: Use when loading high-resolution spatial transcriptomics data (e.g., MERSCOPE, MERFISH, or 10X Xenium cells) or scRNA-seq test data prior to CeLEry training/prediction, and cells with sparse UMIs or few detected genes would otherwise add noise to expression-to-location learning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-quality-filtering-umi-counts))
- **cell-querying-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when reproducing or extending runtime scalability benchmarks of cell-querying methods (Cell BLAST, scmap, CellFishing.jl) against reference datasets spanning 1,000 to 1,000,000 cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-querying-runtime-benchmarking))
- **cell-radial-offset-preservation**: Use when mapping per-cell x/y coordinates from a CODEX (or 10x spatial transcriptomics) image of Swiss-rolled intestinal tissue onto a linear backbone, and you must retain radial (thickness) position, not just along-line position. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-radial-offset-preservation))
- **cell-ranger-barcode-counting**: Use when the input is a 10x Genomics short-read BAM (or FASTQs that will be processed with Cell Ranger) from a hybrid LR-SR scRNA-seq experiment and you need the top SR cellular barcodes that cover most reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-ranger-barcode-counting))
- **cell-rebuttal**: Use after Cell reviews arrive to triage the decision, prioritize the (often substantial) new experiments by impact × feasibility, and draft a point-by-point response that is respectful, evidence-led, and closes the "completeness" gaps reviewers demand. Do not run before the experiments and revision are actually done. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cell-Skills/skills/cell-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)

- **cell-type-aggregation-of-per-cell-scores**: Use when per-cell stemness scores (ORIGINS activity, LandSCENT SR, CytoTRACE) have been computed on annotated scRNA-seq data and you need per-type averages and rankings to benchmark which method correctly identifies stem cells. - **cell-type-annotated-reference-construction**: Use when a large cell-type-annotated scRNA-seq reference (.h5ad) must be reduced to a representative sketch to serve as the reference matrix for deconvolution of pseudobulk/bulk RNA-seq samples, and sketch quality must be judged by proportion-estimate accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotated-reference-construction))
- **cell-type-annotation**: scRNA-seq cell type annotation - read before any annotation skills. Chooses and runs the route: name clusters manually from canonical markers (scanpy, runnable code here), transfer labels with CellTypist or a popV/scANVI on a reference, or do both and validate one against other. Covers package availability, tissue- and state-matched references, marker panels, confidence and marker validation of transferred labels, doublets, unresolved clusters. Tool-specific code lives in celltypist/popv-cell-annotation and scvi-tools-single-cell. ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genomics-bioinformatics/single-cell/cell-type-annotation) · ★394 · 3 copies)
- **cell-type-annotation**: Generate code to assign cell type labels to single-cell RNA-seq clusters using CellTypist, SingleR, marker-based annotation, or reference label transfer (scANVI/ingest). Triggers on requests to "annotate cell types", "label clusters", "run CellTypist", "SingleR annotation", "marker gene dotplot", "transfer labels from reference atlas", "cell identity", "automated annotation", "reference mapping", "scANVI label transfer", "canonical markers", "immune cell types", "hierarchical annotation", "majority voting CellTypist", "over-clustering annotation". ([awslabs/hcls-agent-skills](https://github.com/awslabs/hcls-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-type-annotation) · ★76)
- **cell-type-annotation**: Evidence-driven cell-type annotation of pre-clustered single-cell or spatial transcriptomics data (AnnData). Use whenever the user wants to annotate, label or identify the cell types or states of Leiden/Louvain clusters, build or refine a cell-type hierarchy, or interpret scRNA-seq / snRNA-seq / MERSCOPE / Xenium / Visium / CosMx clusters, even if they just say "what are these clusters?" or "label these cells". Claude is the annotator: it gathers marker evidence, runs one routed automated cross-check, reasons level by level down a hierarchy, has the user sign off its assumptions and critic su… ([quadbio/cell-type-annotation](https://github.com/quadbio/cell-type-annotation/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-type-annotation) · ★5)
- **cell-type-annotation-accuracy-comparison**: Use when a labeled reference scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., GEO/E-MTAB pancreas, PBMC) must be used to annotate one or more independent testing studies and per-method annotation accuracy needs to be quantified across all train-test combinations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-accuracy-comparison))
- **cell-type-annotation-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when a trained cell-type annotation model (e.g. a DNN on .h5ad scRNA-seq data) must be scored against ground-truth labels on a held-out test set, or when comparing annotation tools for accuracy and run feasibility on large datasets (&gt;0.6M cells). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-accuracy-evaluation))
- **cell-type-annotation-accuracy-validation**: Use when GeneVector phenotype_probability annotations must be trusted before downstream analyses like latent-space arithmetic; trigger when an independent label source (e.g., CellAssign) exists for concordance checking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-accuracy-validation))
- **cell-type-annotation-benchmarking**: Use when reproducing Table 1-style accuracy comparisons of cell type annotators across multiple tissue scRNA-seq datasets, or when evaluating how sequencing depth affects single-cell annotation accuracy on a labeled test set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-annotation-by-gsea**: Use when you have Seurat cluster marker lists (only.pos=TRUE, min.pct=0.25, logfc.threshold=0.25) from scRNA-seq data and need automated, database-backed cell-type annotation for each cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-by-gsea))
- **cell-type-annotation-from-marker-genes**: Use when scRNA-seq cluster assignments produced by an unsupervised method (e.g. scBGEDA) lack cell-type labels and a curated marker database such as CellMark can be matched against per-cluster DEGs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-from-marker-genes))
- **cell-type-annotation-from-signature-scores**: Use when a normalized scRNA-seq object (e.g., SingCellaR Human_HSPC) needs cell-type labels from lineage signature gene sets rather than marker-gene inspection or reference mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-from-signature-scores))
- **cell-type-annotation-mapping**: Use when you have a rooted lineage tree in Newick plus per-cell cell-type labels and want to quantify which cell types co-occur within subtrees (e.g., after cutting the LinRace embryo tree at depth 26). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-mapping--7630a66c05bec4ed))
- **cell-type-annotation-mapping**: Use when a single-cell expression matrix (genes x cells) must be paired with a cell-annotation metafile before running NATMI ExtractEdges.py, or when cell-type labels need to be validated so edges are attributed to the right cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-mapping--f89feabe09b6762f))
- **cell-type-annotation-marker-genes**: Use when you have a cell-by-factor or low-dimensional embedding from paired multi-omics data (e.g., Mowgli on TEA-seq PBMC) and need per-cell type labels grounded in canonical gene/protein markers plus an independent reference annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-marker-genes))
- **cell-type-annotation-model-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a cell type annotation tool's accuracy on reference-to-query label transfer, or when assessing whether annotation performance depends on expert-knowledge masks vs. parameter-matched random masks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-model-evaluation))
- **cell-type-annotation-model-training**: Use when confident cell type labels from a consensus of annotation tools must be spread to unlabeled cells in scRNA-seq data via a graph convolutional network, or when tuning GCN hyperparameters per dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-model-training--b47d8a124ee0b25f))
- **cell-type-annotation-model-training**: Use when benchmarking how subsampling/sketching (e.g. scValue sketches at 0.02–0.10 fractions) affects supervised cell-type annotation accuracy on .h5ad scRNA-seq datasets with known ground-truth labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-model-training--b25b15dbb1c13775))
- **cell-type-annotation-pipeline-comparison-benchmarking**: Use when you must quantify resource/accuracy trade-offs of cell type annotation pipelines on identical feature subsets of an scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., reproducing a Table-3-style comparison of accuracy, memory, and execution time). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-pipeline-comparison-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-annotation-plotting**: Use when comparing imputed vs. unimputed scRNA-seq matrices (e.g., Jurkat-293T or Zeisel datasets) to assess whether imputation improves clustering of annotated cell-type subpopulations, as in Fig. 5 of the AutoImpute paper. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-plotting))
- **cell-type-annotation-scoring-against-reference-database**: Use when scRNA-seq clusters (e.g., from Seurat) need automatic, knowledge-free cell type annotation by scoring cluster marker genes against a tissue-specific cell taxonomy reference database rather than manual marker inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-scoring-against-reference-database))
- **cell-type-annotation-standardization**: Use when combining scRNA-seq studies whose free-text cell annotations differ, e.g. before rendering a cross-study comparison of expression across aligned cell types in Cellenium (H5AD-imported studies with ontologized metadata). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-standardization))
- **cell-type-annotation-transfer-with-mlp-classifier**: Use when semi-profiled single-cell data (e.g. scSemiProfiler output for a COVID-19 cohort with Healthy/Asymptomatic/Mild/Moderate/Severe/Critical conditions) is unlabeled, and annotated representative single-cell reference data exists to train a classifier for label transfer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-transfer-with-mlp-classifier))
- **cell-type-annotation-visualization**: Reference for annotating scywalker long-read single-nuclei per-cell gene counts with cell types from a markerset and visualizing the assignments on UMAP in the QC report. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-type-annotation-visualization))
- **cell-type-balanced-subset-allocation**: Use when a large scRNA-seq AnnData (e.g. a 428k-cell gut atlas in .h5ad) must be subsampled to a fixed sketch size while preserving cell-type diversity and rare populations for downstream annotation/ML tasks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-balanced-subset-allocation))
- **cell-type-biomarker-ranking-extraction**: Use when a trained scRNA-seq cell-type classifier (e.g. scaLR DNN on anndata .h5ad data) must be converted into per-class ranked gene lists, or when top-100 biomarkers must be checked for recall against a curated reference like CellMarker2.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-biomarker-ranking-extraction))
- **cell-type-bootstrap-association-testing**: Use when GWAS summary statistics and an annotated single-cell Seurat object are available and the goal is to rank predefined cell types for trait/disease association at FDR &lt; 0.05 (e.g., AD vs. OPCs/microglia, severe COVID-19 vs. PBMC subsets). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-bootstrap-association-testing))
- **cell-type-classification-accuracy-assessment**: Use when scRNA-seq clusters have been produced and reference cell-type labels exist, and you need ARI plus per-cell-type classification accuracy (e.g., alpha/beta cells) to quantify clustering fidelity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-accuracy-assessment))
- **cell-type-classification-accuracy-computation**: Use when benchmarking supervised cell-type classifiers on annotated scRNA-seq data and you need repeatable accuracy and balanced-accuracy estimates for SciBet, scmap, and Seurat v3 across many datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-accuracy-computation))
- **cell-type-classification-evaluation**: Use when a labeled single-cell RNA-seq AnnData (cell x gene, 'class_label' column) needs automated cell-type label transfer with measurable accuracy/F1, including imbalanced, many-class (e.g., 42-type mouse brain) or small-training-set settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-evaluation--942de924bfb6c47e))
- **cell-type-classification-evaluation**: Use when you have a set of k selected marker genes on a labeled scRNA-seq AnnData object and need to quantify how well those markers discriminate cell types via classifier performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-evaluation--b06477fbcf578dba))
- **cell-type-classification-macro-f1-evaluation**: Use when comparing cell type annotation models on a held-out test set and you need donor-generalizing performance metrics such as macro F1-score and cross-entropy loss. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-macro-f1-evaluation))
- **cell-type-classification-performance-evaluation**: Use when you have trained cell-type CRE classifiers (e.g., BOM/XGBoost on motif counts) and need to quantify prediction accuracy, such as fraction of elements assigned to the correct cell type or cross-species transfer rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-performance-evaluation))
- **cell-type-classification-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing or benchmarking the running time of automatic scRNA-seq cell-type identification methods on subsets of varying training/testing sizes, e.g. reproducing the Fig. 4 speed ranking on the mouse brain dataset GSE116470. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-runtime-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-clustering-ari-benchmarking**: Use when reproducing the LUAD/HCC827-H1975-H2228 scRNA-seq clustering benchmark across three platforms with the 2267 common HVGs, or when comparing ARI of unsupervised batch-correction clustering to published values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-clustering-ari-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-clustering-assessment**: Use when an scRNA-Seq matrix has been corrected for the cell-cycle effect and you must verify that cell-type signal was retained while cell-cycle heterogeneity was removed, comparing original vs corrected matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-clustering-assessment))
- **cell-type-clustering-unknown-subtypes**: Use when raw scRNA-seq read count matrices from multiple batches contain cell types that are unknown a priori, and clustering plus batch-effect correction must be performed jointly without preliminary normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-clustering-unknown-subtypes))
- **cell-type-colored-embedding-visualization**: Use when a 2D ivis embedding of scRNA-seq data has been computed and you need to visually validate cell-type segregation and global cluster relationships against published figures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-colored-embedding-visualization))
- **cell-type-comparative-epigenomics**: Use when CpG-level methylation calls (e.g., Roadmap WGBS) must be summarized per interval set and compared across cell types to test differential methylation level/variation between HOT regions and control regions like non-HOT CpG islands. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-comparative-epigenomics))
- **cell-type-composition-input-construction**: Use when preparing seq-based ST data (e.g., 10X Visium slices such as the 12 human DLPFC slices) for Splane spatial domain identification, which requires per-spot cell type composition rather than gene expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-composition-input-construction))
- **cell-type-composition-quantification-across-conditions**: Use when comparing cell-state or cell-type proportions between conditions (e.g., KO vs WT) in scRNA-seq data where cells have been projected onto a reference atlas and assigned discrete cell-state labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-composition-quantification-across-conditions))
- **cell-type-composition-regression-analysis**: Use when a single-cell/nucleus atlas has cell-type or cell-state annotations plus per-donor covariates, and the goal is to test whether cell-type fractions differ across a condition (e.g. obesity, depot) while controlling for covariates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-composition-regression-analysis))
- **cell-type-consistency-evaluation**: Use when comparing cell type annotation accuracy of cluster-based vs cell-based methods on scRNA-seq datasets with known reference labels, or when quantifying how swapping the underlying reference database affects annotation consistency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-consistency-evaluation))
- **cell-type-consistency-rate-computation**: Use when evaluating how accurately an automatic cell-type annotation method labels clusters of scRNA-seq data against manually curated cell types, and a comparable consistent-rate table across multiple methods is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-consistency-rate-computation))
- **cell-type-correlation-strategy-selection**: Use when training a supervised single-cell label-assignment model on scRNA-seq data with known cell types and you must decide between a flat vs hierarchical classifier based on how highly correlated the training cell types are. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-correlation-strategy-selection))
- **cell-type-correspondence-mapping**: Use when visualizing two or more cellular-resolution SRT sections jointly (e.g., Stereo-seq axolotl DPI-10 vs DPI-15) and cell-type labels differ across sections, so naive default palettes misalign colors between biologically related or confusable cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-correspondence-mapping))
- **cell-type-count-aggregation**: Use when you have a cell-by-feature scRNA-seq or scATAC-seq count matrix with per-cell type labels and need across-cell-type expression/accessibility profiles for Spearman/k-medoids clustering or Kendall's Tb correlation validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-count-aggregation))
- **cell-type-count-matrix-aggregation**: Use when a single-cell count matrix (e.g., scRNA-seq from MOSim sc_mosim) must be reduced to per-cell-type average profiles before clustering, PCA, or cross-omics correlation validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-type-count-matrix-aggregation))
- **cell-type-deconvolution-method-evaluation**: Use when comparing cell type identification or gene expression decomposition methods on simulated single-slice spatial transcriptomics benchmarks with paired scRNA-seq references and ground-truth per-cell assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-deconvolution-method-evaluation))
- **cell-type-deconvolution-wnnls**: Use when estimating cell type proportions from bulk RNA-seq read counts with a multi-subject single-cell reference, especially for closely related cell types and without pre-selected marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-deconvolution-wnnls))
- **cell-type-differential-abundance-testing**: Use when a samples-by-features LC/MS intensity matrix (raw or batch-corrected) has group labels and the goal is to rank or count metabolomic features that differ between groups at a relaxed FDR threshold such as ≤ 0.2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cell-type-differential-abundance-testing))
- **cell-type-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when a single-cell dataset contains annotated cell types and two conditions, and you need per-cell-type DE gene counts and magnitudes to rank the biological response across populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-differential-expression-analysis))
- **cell-type-disjoint-cross-validation-splitting**: Use when benchmarking cross-modality translation methods (e.g., scRNA-seq to scATAC-seq) for generalization to cell types absent from training, mimicking the MB/MCC/MK/PBMC unseen-cell-type evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-disjoint-cross-validation-splitting))
- **cell-type-drug-response-ranking**: Use when you need to identify which cell type in an untreated disease scRNA-seq dataset is most responsive to a drug with a known target gene, without paired treated/untreated samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-drug-response-ranking))
- **cell-type-enrichment-analysis**: Use when SINCERA hierarchical clustering has produced cell clusters and each cluster must be annotated with a predicted cell type using differentially expressed genes and a Fisher's exact test against the EBI Expression Atlas annotation table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-enrichment-analysis))
- **cell-type-entropy-coefficient-of-variation-benchmarking**: Use when comparing segmentation methods on spatial transcriptomics data (e.g., MERSCOPE melanoma, CosMx lung) where no pixel-level ground truth exists and you need reference-based homogeneity vs cell-size consistency metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-entropy-coefficient-of-variation-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-epiallele-trajectory-comparison**: Use when comparing methylated-core/epiallele structures across purified cell populations (e.g., astrocytes, neurons, MOEs from cerebellum and cortex) to test for shared precursor cores during postnatal differentiation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-epiallele-trajectory-comparison))
- **cell-type-expression-aggregation**: Use when a scRNA-seq study stored as an AnnData/H5AD object must be loaded into a relational sparse expression table and queried for gene-level per-cell-type averages, e.g. to replicate Cellenium's server-side aggregation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-expression-aggregation))
- **cell-type-expression-specificity-ranking**: Use when a query gene of interest (e.g. 'insulin') must be matched to the annotated cell types that specifically express it, within one study or across multiple ingested scRNA-seq studies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-expression-specificity-ranking))
- **cell-type-f1-score-computation**: Use when comparing label-transfer accuracy of scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq integration methods on cells with held-out ground-truth annotations, especially under varying annotation budgets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-f1-score-computation))
- **cell-type-flux-differential-testing**: Use when per-cell metabolic fluxes have been predicted from scRNA-seq (scFEA) and must be compared across reported cell-type labels (e.g. cancer, myeloid, T cells) to test which population has the highest glucose metabolic rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cell-type-flux-differential-testing))
- **cell-type-fraction-heatmap-visualization**: Use when bulk RNA-seq samples have been deconvolved against a single-cell reference (e.g., via scPlant decomposeBulk on an annotated Seurat object) and you need a heatmap of per-sample cell-type fractions to compare compositions across conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-fraction-heatmap-visualization))
- **cell-type-functional-annotation**: Use when an unsupervised clustering (e.g., Leiden) of scRNA-seq data has produced clusters with no labels and you must assign a cell type from the functional enrichment of cluster-specific genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-functional-annotation))
- **cell-type-gene-signature-definition**: Use when cell types have been annotated across merged datasets (e.g., after Harmony-kernel integration and label propagation) and a unified marker-gene signature per type/subtype is needed, such as neuronal (LINGO2, CUX2, SST) and glial (SLC1A2, MBP, CLDN5) markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-gene-signature-definition))
- **cell-type-ground-truth-precision-evaluation**: Use when a real scRNA-seq dataset has trusted cell-type labels serving as ground truth and you need to quantify how precisely a gene-set or pathway score (TRS, scDRS) retrieves the trait-relevant cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-ground-truth-precision-evaluation))
- **cell-type-held-out-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking scRNA-seq integration methods on two datasets with known cell-type compositions and you want to verify that a cell type present in only one dataset is left unmatched instead of incorrectly aligned. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-held-out-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-hierarchy-construction**: Use when training a custom Census cell-type annotation model from a scRNA-seq reference (e.g. Tabula Sapiens plus CCLE) that requires inferring the cell-type hierarchy and fitting node classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-hierarchy-construction))
- **cell-type-homology-annotation**: Use when integrating single-cell datasets from multiple species whose cell type annotations use different or dataset-specific ontology terms, and a shared cell type homology mapping is required before integration and scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-homology-annotation))
- **cell-type-hop-distance-computation**: Use when you have an annotated SpatialData object (cell boundaries, cell-type labels, niche assignments) and need to test whether specific cell populations exclusively interact with themselves or are spatially restricted to particular niches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-type-hop-distance-computation))
- **cell-type-identification-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether joint (all-slices) inference improves cell type labeling accuracy of spatial transcriptomics methods, or when comparing ST cell type identification methods on simulated Visium data with known ground-truth single-cell labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-identification-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-identification-performance-metrics**: Use when comparing or tuning a scRNA-seq cell type identifier on annotated datasets and you need to quantify correct/erroneous assignments for known and unknown cell populations (C, E, EA, CUA, EUA). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-identification-performance-metrics))
- **cell-type-identification-potts-model-map-inference**: Use when you need per-cell (not per-spot) discrete cell type labels for ST spots (Visium, Slide-seq), given nucleus segmentation outputs and a labeled scRNA-seq reference, with spatial smoothness enforced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-identification-potts-model-map-inference))
- **cell-type-importance-visualization**: Use when you have dsCellNet-inferred ligand-receptor networks across multiple developmental time points (e.g., GSE153164 corticogenesis stages) and need per-cell-type incoming/outgoing/total edge counts to rank the most communicating cell types per stage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-importance-visualization))
- **cell-type-interaction-overlap-statistics**: Use when gABC/ABC-scored enhancer-gene interactions have been predicted per cell type from snATAC-seq candidate enhancers and you need per-cell-type interaction counts plus cross-cell-type overlap/shared fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-interaction-overlap-statistics))
- **cell-type-isoform-usage-aggregation**: Use when SingleCellExperiment 3'UTR isoform count matrices (*.txs.Rds, *.genes.Rds) from scUTRquant must be summarized per cell type and genes labeled multi-UTR vs IPA under a ≥10% isoform-fraction cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-isoform-usage-aggregation))
- **cell-type-isoform-usage-analysis**: Use when single-cell PacBio Iso-Seq data must be compared across cell types to identify which isoforms (e.g., ENST transcripts) dominate per cell type, or to test whether a gene undergoes major isoform switching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-type-isoform-usage-analysis))
- **cell-type-label-alignment-evaluation**: Use when you have per-cell embeddings (e.g., tGPT transformer features) plus ground-truth cell type annotations and must quantify clustering alignment via NMI, ARI, and FMI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-alignment-evaluation))
- **cell-type-label-guided-bayesian-matrix-factorization**: Use when scRNA-seq count matrices come with trusted cell type labels and the goal is dropout imputation that leverages those labels, e.g. benchmarking silhouette/Pearson clustering metrics against scImpute, DrImpute, PBLR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-guided-bayesian-matrix-factorization))
- **cell-type-label-hierarchy-classification**: Use when benchmarking marker gene panels against a two-layer cell-type label hierarchy on scRNA-seq data, or when testing whether fewer hierarchy-aware markers match a one-vs-all baseline panel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-hierarchy-classification))
- **cell-type-label-permutation-ablation**: Use when benchmarking NeuronChat's projection prediction on retrograde-labeling ground truth and you must rule out that benchmark performance is driven by node cell-type labels rather than the specific ground-truth graph topology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-permutation-ablation))
- **cell-type-label-permutation-testing**: Use when constructing a cell–cell communication network from scRNA-seq data and you need to decide which sender–receiver L–R interactions are statistically significant rather than keeping all expression-product edges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-permutation-testing))
- **cell-type-label-prediction**: Use when annotating cell types in scRNA-seq data without training on the test dataset, or when benchmarking a zero-shot label predictor against supervised classifiers like CellTypist on a multi-batch atlas such as the open-problems pancreas dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-prediction))
- **cell-type-label-propagation-across-datasets**: Use when multiple independent scRNA-seq datasets (e.g., three cortex studies totaling ~105,099 cells) are jointly integrated and one or more reference annotations must be propagated to unlabeled cells without re-clustering from scratch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-propagation-across-datasets))
- **cell-type-label-supervised-feature-ranking**: Use when building a Cellcano reference for scATAC-seq celltyping: the reference gene score matrix (from ArchR) has annotated cell type labels and features must be reduced to cell-type-specific genes before MLP training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-supervised-feature-ranking))
- **cell-type-marker-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a cell-type-specific gene network (weighted edge matrix) for one of 26 cell types and need to verify that its node scores recover the correct pangaloDB cell-type markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-enrichment-analysis))
- **cell-type-marker-enrichment-annotation**: Use when scQA clustering (labels.txt) has assigned cells to clusters and you must confirm each cluster's key genes overlap known markers, hub genes, and DEGs (e.g., endothelial ADTRP, CDH5, CLEC14A, ACVRL1). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-enrichment-annotation))
- **cell-type-marker-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when you have a gene marker list selected from scRNA-seq phenotypes and need to verify it recovers the correct tissues and cell types by enrichment against a curated reference set such as HuBMAP_ASCTplusB_augmented_2022. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-enrichment-interpretation))
- **cell-type-marker-gene-matrix-handling**: Use when reconstructing or fine-tuning the DCNet network and you need to wire 9078 marker genes to 434 cell-type hidden neurons via the zero-one relation matrix and align input/output neuron ordering with index.pkl. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-gene-matrix-handling))
- **cell-type-marker-gene-selection**: Use when you have a raw/normalized scRNA-seq count matrix paired with manually annotated cell-type labels and need a per-cell-type positive marker gene list to drive marker-based or marker-learning cell-type prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-gene-selection))
- **cell-type-marker-gene-validation**: Use when scBridge (or a baseline such as scJoint, Seurat or GLUE) has produced predicted cell-type labels on target scATAC/ADT data and you need marker-based evidence that the predicted populations (e.g. Naive vs Effector CD4+ T cells) are biologically correct. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-gene-validation))
- **cell-type-marker-gmv-inference**: Use when you have an annotated single-cell dataset (e.g., the Field et al. week-2 cortical organoid data, GEO GSE106245) plus a cell type marker GMT file and want interpretable, module-level per-cell cell type activity rather than opaque clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-gmv-inference))
- **cell-type-marker-validation**: Use when a single-cell TE matrix (scTE .h5ad output from scRNA-seq or scTEATAC output from scATAC-seq) has been clustered and you must confirm cell-type assignments or marker-TE specificity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-validation))
- **cell-type-marker-verification-from-loadings**: Use when a scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g. SingleCellExperiment) has been embedded with correspondence analysis and you want to check whether high-loading genes correspond to known cell-type markers (e.g. GNLY, TYROBP, LYZ, S100A8, CD74, HLA-DRA) and interpret cell-population associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-verification-from-loadings))
- **cell-type-matching-metric-evaluation**: Use when comparing cross-species cell type matching pipelines (e.g., human vs mouse vs marmoset primary motor cortex scRNA-seq) and you need reproducible ADS/recall metrics, including gene-matching ablations that test whether ProtBERT gene matches outperform BLAST matches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-matching-metric-evaluation))
- **cell-type-mean-expression-profile-computation**: Use when preparing labelled scRNA-seq training data (e.g. SingleCellExperiment objects with known cell labels) and you need per-cell-type mean expression profiles plus a cross-cell-type correlation matrix to decide a classifier strategy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-mean-expression-profile-computation))
- **cell-type-neighborhood-enrichment-analysis**: Use when single-cell data have been pseudo-spatially mapped onto ST spots (e.g., via STEM's netsc2st mapping) and the goal is to quantify spatial proximity/colocalization between rare cell types such as TSKs, endothelial cells, or pDCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-neighborhood-enrichment-analysis))
- **cell-type-neighbourhood-proportion-computation**: Use when comparing the fidelity of cell-to-cell neighbourhood (cell-cell association) structure between two spatial transcriptomics representations — e.g. bin2cell-reconstructed cells vs default 8 μm Visium HD bins — against an expert-annotated MERFISH reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-type-neighbourhood-proportion-computation))
- **cell-type-nomenclature-standardization**: Use when combining marker gene sets from multiple published databases or cell-sorting sources whose gene symbols and cell type labels follow non-standard, inconsistent nomenclature, and you need harmonized, comparable sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-nomenclature-standardization))
- **cell-type-perturbation-detection**: Use when you have a normalized scRNA-seq count matrix with per-cell metadata assigning condition, patient, and cluster, and need to identify which predefined cell types are transcriptomically perturbed between two conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-perturbation-detection))
- **cell-type-perturbation-ranking**: Use when you have untreated diseased scRNA-seq data with cell-type annotations and a known drug target gene, and need to identify which cell types are drug-responsive or resistant without paired treated/untreated samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-perturbation-ranking))
- **cell-type-phenotype-association-testing**: Use when cell-type proportions have been estimated from bulk RNA-seq (via BisqueRNA bulk.props or BisqueMarker) and you need to test associations with phenotypes such as BMI, Matsuda index, or Braak stage, replicating reported cell-composition associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-phenotype-association-testing))
- **cell-type-positive-fraction-quantification**: Use when single-cell AnnData atlases spanning multiple ages (e.g., Tabula Muris Senis, 328K cells, 19 tissues, 1-30 months) must be scored for senescence and aggregated into per-cell-type positive-cell proportions or old/young ratios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-positive-fraction-quantification))
- **cell-type-potency-ranking-analysis**: Use when a scRNA-Seq count matrix with malignant/non-malignant or cell-type annotations is available and the goal is to estimate and rank differentiation potency of cell types without marker genes, feature selection, or training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-potency-ranking-analysis))
- **cell-type-prediction-confusion-matrix-computation**: Use when you have Cellcano (or similar supervised) predicted cell-type labels for a target scATAC-seq dataset plus ground-truth labels (e.g., FACS-sorted FACS labels embedded in cell identifiers) and need to quantify per-class prediction accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-prediction-confusion-matrix-computation))
- **cell-type-probability-classification**: Use when single-cell expression data has a small labeled training set and you need probabilistic cell type predictions plus predictive entropy to guide cell selection or evaluate annotation uncertainty. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-probability-classification))
- **cell-type-probability-expression-correlation**: Use when you need marker genes for cell types absent from training data, given OnClass prediction matrices (pred_Y_all.released.npy) and single-cell expression data such as Tabula Muris Senis FACS cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-probability-expression-correlation))
- **cell-type-profile-matching**: Use when ST pixels are cell mixtures and you need to assign reference-free deconvolved cell-type transcriptional profiles to known cell populations and compare them across conditions (e.g., aged vs young) for perturbation-driven gene changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-profile-matching))
- **cell-type-proportion-accuracy-evaluation**: Decision support for validating false-positive control of Scanpro proportion analysis on null simulations, and for choosing pseudo-replicate counts (2–14) based on total cell counts per condition (~1,000 to ~50,000 cells). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-proportion-accuracy-evaluation))
- **cell-type-proportion-computation-per-condition**: Use when comparing cell-type composition of an AI-generated (semi-profiled) cohort against real-profiled single-cell ground truth across defined conditions, e.g. COVID-19 severity states (Healthy, Asymptomatic, Mild, Moderate, Severe, Critical).
